Ruling: model error; adjudicates the declaration, not the tax reading. Not executable: the reply fails validation (person.h_se_line4a_mult: "declared NonNegDollars but the expression does not prove non-negativity"), so triage cannot run any of its lines (triage/person.deepseek.json, state invalid); this line's candidate reaches person.h_se_line4a_mult (92.35% of its Schedule SE line 3, which is Schedule C line 31 and can be a loss; its guard uses the product only when line 3 is above zero, but the declared type is not proved) through person.ira_line6, so no value of it was computed. By reading, two errors against i1040gi pp. 97-98: (1) line 6: "Is the amount on line 5 less than the amount on line 2? No. STOP None of your IRA contributions are deductible"; the reply's line 6 is zero then and its tiers enter the $200 minimum ("If the result is $200 or more, enter the result. But if it is less than $200, enter $200" applies only to a person who reached line 7); (2) for a joint return where one spouse is covered, the column of the person not covered goes through lines 2 to 7 with $246,000, the $10,000 threshold and 70% ("in the column for the IRA of the person who wasn't covered by a retirement plan, multiply by 70% (0.70)"); the reply enters the full limit for every person not covered, so that spouse keeps $7,000 inside the $236,000 to $246,000 phase-out.

By reading: i1040gi p. 98, line 8: "Enter the total of your (and your spouse's if filing jointly): Wages, salaries, tips, etc.", line 10 adds lines 8 and 9, and line 12a is "the smallest of line 7a, 10, or 11a". The reply limits each person by that person's own box 1 and self-employment amount, not the joint line 10, so on a joint return a spouse with no earnings gets no deduction (the spousal IRA the combined line 10 allows); it also inherits its line 7's errors.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: person.employers
    printed: "employers"
    label: "Number of employers"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one}
  - id: person.overcount
    printed: "overcount"
    label: "Number of employers that withheld more than the Social Security tax limit"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one, where: {op: test, gt: [w2.box4, {param: ss.limit}]}}
  - id: person.over
    printed: "over"
    label: "Any employer withheld more than the Social Security tax limit"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.overcount, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: person.wages
    printed: "wages"
    label: "Social Security wages and tips"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_w2_box3_total, person.h_w2_box7_total]}
  - id: person.withheld
    printed: "withheld"
    label: "Social Security tax withheld"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box4}
  - id: person.eligible
    printed: "eligible"
    label: "Eligible for the excess Social Security credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.employers, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.wages, {param: ss.base}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.over, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.excess
    printed: "excess"
    label: "Excess Social Security tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ss_excess}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.zero
    printed: "zero"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: person.credit
    printed: "credit"
    label: "Excess Social Security credit for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.excess}
    refuses: [person.rrta]
  - id: person.sep_s1
    printed: "Step 1"
    label: "Net profit from Schedule C"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31}
  - id: person.sep_s2
    printed: "Step 2"
    label: "Deduction for self-employment tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.se_line13}
  - id: person.sep_s3
    printed: "Step 3"
    label: "Net earnings from self-employment"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.sep_s1, take: person.sep_s2}
  - id: person.sep_s5
    printed: "Step 5"
    label: "Net earnings multiplied by the self-employed rate"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sep_s5}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.sep_s6
    printed: "Step 6"
    label: "Compensation limit multiplied by the plan contribution rate"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sep_s6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.sep_s7
    printed: "Step 7"
    label: "Smaller of Step 5 or Step 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sep_s7}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.sep_s8
    printed: "Step 8"
    label: "Contribution dollar limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sep_s8_calc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.sep_s21
    printed: "Step 21"
    label: "Maximum deductible contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sep_max}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.plan_screen]
  - id: person.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses for the American opportunity credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc_eligible, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_line27_calc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc_eligible, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.edu_over]
  - id: person.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Line 27 less $2,000, not below zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.edu_line27, take: {const: 200000}}
  - id: person.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Line 28 multiplied by 25%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
  - id: person.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "American opportunity credit allowed for this student"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: person.edu_line27}
        - {guard: {op: test, ne: [person.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: person.h_edu_line30_add}
  - id: person.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses for the lifetime learning credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc_eligible, {const: true}]}, then: person.zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc_ok, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_llc_calc}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc_eligible, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc_ok, {const: false}]}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.edu_over]

  - id: person.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: person.one}
  - id: person.educator_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Educator expense deduction for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_exp_cap}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.h_edu_exp_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Educator expenses limited to $300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.educator_expenses, person.h_s1_educator_max]}
  - id: person.h_s1_educator_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Educator expense maximum"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: s1.educator_max}
  - id: person.hsa_forms
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of HSA forms"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one}
  - id: person.hsa_split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of HSA forms with a spouse allocation"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one, where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.hsa_agreed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of HSA forms using an agreed allocation"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one, where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}}
  - id: person.hsa_step1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "HSA Step 1 total"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.step1}
  - id: person.hsa_share
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "HSA allocated share"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.share}
  - id: person.hsa_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "HSA deduction"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line13}
  - id: person.hsa_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable HSA distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line16}
  - id: person.hsa_addtax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional 20% tax on HSA distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line17b}
  - id: person.tip_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers with qualified tips"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.one}
  - id: person.tip_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total qualified tips"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.col_d}
  - id: person.tip_reported
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified tips reported by employers"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.amount}
  - id: person.ot_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers with qualified overtime"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.one}
  - id: person.ot_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total qualified overtime compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.col_b}
  - id: person.box1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total Form W-2 box 1 wages"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box1}
  - id: person.box2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total Form W-2 box 2 federal income tax withheld"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box2}
  - id: person.medicare5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total Form W-2 box 5 Medicare wages and tips"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box5}
  - id: person.medicare6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total Form W-2 box 6 Medicare tax withheld"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box6}
  - id: person.sc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedule C businesses"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one}
  - id: person.ho_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedule C businesses with home office use"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one, where: {op: test, eq: [sc.ho_uses, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.mq_q4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Mid-quarter fourth-quarter depreciable basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_q4}
  - id: person.mq_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Mid-quarter total depreciable basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_total}
  - id: person.cost179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Section 179 cost"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.cost179}
  - id: person.s179_nonlisted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Section 179 allowed for nonlisted property"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.s179_allowed}
  - id: person.s179_listed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Section 179 allowed for listed property"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.zero}
    note: "Listed-property section 179 is outside the supplied asset screening interface; this aggregate carries zero."
  - id: person.income179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Business income for the section 179 limit"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.sc_total}
  - id: person.sc_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total Schedule C net profit or loss"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31}
  - id: person.se_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 6 net earnings from self-employment"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_se_line4c_pos}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 12 self-employment tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_se_line12}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 13 deduction for one-half of self-employment tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.se_line12, rate: {param: se.rate_50}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.s16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed SEP, SIMPLE and qualified plan deduction"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_sep, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sep_actual}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_simple, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_simple_total}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_plan_none, {const: true}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.plan_screen]
  - id: person.positive_profits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total positive Schedule C profits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.profit_pos}
  - id: person.sehi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed health insurance deduction"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.f7206_line14}
  - id: person.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified business income deduction from Schedule C businesses"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.qbi}
  - id: person.eic_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employment earned income for the earned income credit"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_se_line3, take: person.se_line13}
  - id: person.sc_1a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of statutory-employee Schedule C businesses"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one, where: {op: test, eq: [sc.statutory, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.dc_benefits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent care benefits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.dc_benefits}
  - id: person.dc_has_box10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Any Form W-2 box 10 dependent care benefits"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.h_w2_box10_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: person.dc_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for the child and dependent care credit"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_dc_earned}
  - id: person.dc_earned_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for the child and dependent care credit (second column)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_dc_earned}
    note: "The archived per-person interface does not distinguish two earned-income amounts for one person; both dependent-care earned-income fields carry this person's earned income."
  - id: person.sav_ineligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Ineligible for the retirement savings contributions credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_student, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.sav_line1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Retirement savings contributions credit line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_sav_trad, person.sav_roth]}
    refuses: [person.sav_able]
  - id: person.sav_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Retirement savings contributions credit line 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_sav_deferral_total, person.sav_voluntary]}
    refuses: [person.sav_able, person.sav_excess]
  - id: person.ira_contrib
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA contributions for the year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_trad}
  - id: person.ira_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Covered by a retirement plan at work"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_work_plan}
  - id: person.ira_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction worksheet line 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit_cov}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit_not}
  - id: person.ira_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction worksheet line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_ira_line6_calc}
  - id: person.ira_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction worksheet line 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_below_joint, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}]}, then: person.h_ira_min40}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_below_joint, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}]}, then: person.h_ira_min35}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_below_other, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}]}, then: person.h_ira_min80}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_below_other, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}]}, then: person.h_ira_min70}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_below_joint, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_below_other, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit}
  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_min1, person.ira_contrib]}
    refuses: [person.ira_excess]
  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nondeductible traditional IRA contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.ira_contrib, take: person.ira_deduction}
  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 is required"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.h_ira_line3, {const: 0}]}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_basis_adjusted, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 2 total basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8606, field: f8606.line2}
  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA distributions requiring Form 8606"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}, {op: test, gt: [person.h_ira_line3, {const: 0}]}]}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line14
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 14 total basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8606, field: f8606.line14}
  - id: person.ira_ssb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction used in the social security benefits worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_deduction}
  - id: person.ira_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employment earned income for the IRA deduction worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.se_line6, take: person.h_ira_se_ded}
  - id: person.edu_kid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Refundable American opportunity credit restriction applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_age_kid, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_parent, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_joint, {const: false}]}
    note: "The archived questions do not separately identify age 18; the threshold age questions are used as the closest reading of the under-24 restriction."

  - id: person.h_w2_box3_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total Form W-2 box 3 social security wages"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box3}
  - id: person.h_w2_box7_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total Form W-2 box 7 social security tips"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box7}
  - id: person.h_ss_excess
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Social Security tax withheld over the limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.withheld, take: {param: ss.limit}}
  - id: person.h_plan_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan is a SEP"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "sep"}]}
  - id: person.h_plan_simple
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan is a SIMPLE IRA"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}
  - id: person.h_plan_none
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan is none"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}
  - id: person.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}
  - id: person.h_hundred_cents
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One hundred cents"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.hundred_cents}
  - id: person.h_sep_rate_cents
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP plan rate in cents"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: person.h_one_cent, count: person.sep_rate}
  - id: person.h_sep_whole
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One hundred plus the SEP plan rate"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_hundred_cents, person.h_sep_rate_cents]}
  - id: person.h_ret_comp_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.comp_limit}
  - id: person.h_sep_s5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP Step 5 amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.sep_s3, part: person.h_sep_rate_cents, whole: person.h_sep_whole, places: 6}
  - id: person.h_sep_s6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP Step 6 amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.h_ret_comp_limit, part: person.h_sep_rate_cents, whole: person.h_hundred_cents, places: 6}
  - id: person.h_sep_s7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP Step 7 amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_sep_s5, person.h_sep_s6]}
  - id: person.h_sep_s8_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP contribution dollar limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.sep_dollar_limit}
  - id: person.h_sep_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP maximum deductible contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_s7, person.sep_s8]}
  - id: person.h_sep_actual
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP deduction limited to the maximum"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_contrib, person.sep_s21]}
  - id: person.h_simple_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE IRA deduction"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.simple_deferral, person.simple_employer]}
  - id: person.h_se_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 3"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.sc_total}
  - id: person.h_se_line4a_mult
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 3 multiplied by 92.35%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_se_line3, rate: {param: se.rate_9235}}
  - id: person.h_se_line4a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 4a"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [person.h_se_line3, {const: 0}]}, then: person.h_se_line4a_mult}
        - {guard: {op: test, le: [person.h_se_line3, {const: 0}]}, then: person.h_se_line3}
  - id: person.h_se_line4c_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 4c not below zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_se_line4a, take: person.zero}
  - id: person.h_se_wage_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Social Security wage base"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: se.wage_base}
  - id: person.h_se_line9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 9"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_se_wage_base, take: person.wages}
  - id: person.h_se_line10_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of Schedule SE line 6 or line 9"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.se_line6, person.h_se_line9]}
  - id: person.h_se_line10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_se_line10_base, rate: {param: se.rate_124}}
  - id: person.h_se_line11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.se_line6, rate: {param: se.rate_029}}
  - id: person.h_se_line12_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 12 before the $400 stop"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_se_line10, person.h_se_line11]}
  - id: person.h_se_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE line 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, lt: [person.se_line6, {param: se.floor}]}, then: person.zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [person.se_line6, {param: se.floor}]}, then: person.h_se_line12_raw}
  - id: person.h_ira_se_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employment tax and plan deductions for IRA earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.se_line13, person.s16]}
  - id: person.h_w2_box10_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms W-2 with box 10 dependent care benefits"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one, where: {op: test, eq: [w2.has_box10, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.h_dc_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for dependent care"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.box1, person.eic_se]}
  - id: person.h_sav_trad
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA contributions for the saver's credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: true}]}, then: person.ira_trad}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.h_sav_deferral_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total elective deferrals for the saver's credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.sav_deferral}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 2 for a covered person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit_not
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 2 for a person not covered by a plan"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2_not_covered}
  - id: person.h_ira_line4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 4 adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [s1.line11, s1.line13, s1.line15, s1.line16, s1.line17, s1.line18, s1.line19a, s1.line24a, s1.line25]}
  - id: person.h_ira_line5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1040.line9, take: person.h_ira_line4}
  - id: person.h_ira_line6_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA worksheet line 6 amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.ira_line2, take: person.h_ira_line5}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA contribution limit under age 50"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit50
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA contribution limit age 50 or older"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA contribution limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit50}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit70}
  - id: person.h_ira_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Joint filing status"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "qss"}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_below_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA line 6 below the joint threshold"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 2000000}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_below_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA line 6 below the nonjoint threshold"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 1000000}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA minimum"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}
  - id: person.h_ira_p70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA line 6 multiplied by 70%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA line 6 multiplied by 80%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA line 6 multiplied by 35%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA line 6 multiplied by 40%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}
  - id: person.h_ira_r70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 70% amount rounded up to $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_p70, unit: 1000, rule: up}
  - id: person.h_ira_r80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 80% amount rounded up to $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_p80, unit: 1000, rule: up}
  - id: person.h_ira_r35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 35% amount rounded up to $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_p35, unit: 1000, rule: up}
  - id: person.h_ira_r40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 40% amount rounded up to $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_p40, unit: 1000, rule: up}
  - id: person.h_ira_min70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 70% amount with minimum"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_r70, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.h_ira_min80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 80% amount with minimum"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_r80, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.h_ira_min35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 35% amount with minimum"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_r35, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.h_ira_min40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA 40% amount with minimum"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_r40, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.h_ira_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.box1, person.ira_se]}
  - id: person.h_ira_min1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of IRA line 7 or earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.ira_line7, person.h_ira_earned]}
  - id: person.h_ira_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_nondeductible, person.ira_8606_line2]}
  - id: person.h_edu_age_kid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age condition for the refundable American opportunity credit restriction"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2002, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_fulltime, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_aoc_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Eligible for the American opportunity credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total qualified education expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_inst, person.edu_mat]}
  - id: person.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Education expense adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_a2025, person.edu_a2026, person.edu_refunds]}
  - id: person.h_edu_adjusted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_edu_total, take: person.h_edu_adj}
  - id: person.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit expense cap"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}
  - id: person.h_edu_line27_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit adjusted expenses capped"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_edu_adjusted, person.h_aoc_cap]}
  - id: person.h_aoc_full_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit first band"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}
  - id: person.h_edu_line30_add
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit above the first band"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_aoc_full_band, person.edu_line29]}
  - id: person.h_edu_llc_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lifetime learning credit computation applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc_eligible, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_llc_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lifetime learning credit adjusted expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.edu_inst, take: person.h_edu_adj}
```
